Abstract: Human label variation has been established as a central phenomenon in NLP: the perspectives different annotators have on the same item need to be embraced. Data collection practices thus shifted towards increasing the annotator numbers and releasing disaggregated datasets, harmful language being most resourced due to its high subjectivity. While this resulted in rich information about \textit{who} annotated (sociodemographics, attitudes, etc.), the \textit{what} (e.g., linguistic properties of items), and their interplay has received little attention. We present the first large-scale analysis of four reference datasets for harmful language detection, bringing together annotator characteristics, linguistic properties of the items, and their interactions in a statistically informed picture. We find that interactions are crucial, revealing intersectional effects ignored in previous work, and that a strong role is played by lexical cues and annotator attitudes. Effect patterns, however, vary considerably across datasets. This urges caution about generalization and transferability.

</details>


### [38] [Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools: A Full-Pipeline Recipe for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326)
*Qianjia Cheng,Yuchen Zhang,Zhilin Wang,Yuxin Zuo,Shunkai Zhang,Yuchen Fan,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在强思考模型中注入自然的工具使用行为而不牺牲其纯文本推理能力，提出了一套完整工具集成推理（TIR）方案，包括SFT数据选择、比例控制、优化策略和稳定RL训练，在Qwen3模型上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具集成推理能拓展思考模型的能力边界，但反直觉的是，即使强思考模型几乎不实际调用工具，工具启用的评估反而会降低推理性能。因此需要在不牺牲纯文本推理能力的前提下，向强思考模型注入自然的工具使用行为。

Method: (i) TIR监督微调基于教师轨迹的可学习性，优先选择天然适合工具增强的问题；(ii)控制工具使用轨迹的比例以缓解纯文本推理能力的灾难性遗忘；(iii)优化pass@k和响应长度而非训练损失以最大化TIR SFT收益并保留RL探索空间；(iv)基于合适的SFT初始化和防模式崩溃机制的稳定RLVR训练。

Result: 在Qwen3思考模型的4B和30B规模上，AIME 2025分别达到96.7%和99.2%，在多个基准测试中达到开源模型SOTA。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TIR方案能有效在保持纯文本推理能力的同时注入工具使用能力，在多个规模上取得优异性能。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) offers a direct way to extend thinking models beyond the limits of text-only reasoning. Paradoxically, we observe that tool-enabled evaluation can degrade reasoning performance even when the strong thinking models make almost no actual tool calls. In this paper, we investigate how to inject natural tool-use behavior into a strong thinking model without sacrificing its no-tool reasoning ability, and present a comprehensive TIR recipe. We highlight that (i) the effectiveness of TIR supervised fine-tuning (SFT) hinges on the learnability of teacher trajectories, which should prioritize problems inherently suited for tool-augmented solutions; (ii) controlling the proportion of tool-use trajectories could mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of text-only reasoning capacity; (iii) optimizing for pass@k and response length instead of training loss could maximize TIR SFT gains while preserving headroom for reinforcement learning (RL) exploration; (iv) a stable RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) stage, built upon suitable SFT initialization and explicit safeguards against mode collapse, provides a simple yet remarkably effective solution. When applied to Qwen3 thinking models at 4B and 30B scales, our recipe yields models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in a wide range of benchmarks among open-source models, such as 96.7% and 99.2% on AIME 2025 for 4B and 30B, respectively.

</details>


### [39] [Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，语言模型的安全行为会因任务是否被标记为“评估”而发生显著变化，不同模型表现出“评估谨慎”或“部署谨慎”两种相反模式，且这一异质性依赖于安全评判器的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准测试的结论往往被直接推广到模型部署后的行为，但这种推断可能不成立——如果模型的行为取决于它是否感知到自己在被评估。作者希望系统性地检验这一假设。

Method: 提出“评估-上下文分歧”概念，设计配对提示协议：在控制释义变化、基准熟悉度和评判器框架敏感性的前提下，对同一任务分别施加“评估”、“部署交互”和“中性请求”三种框架，测量行为变化。在4个开源模型家族（含OLMo-3基座与指令微调版本）上进行实验，共20个配对项，每个检查点生成840次。

Result: OLMo-3-Instruct表现出“评估谨慎”（评估框架下拒答率比中性高11.8pp，有害顺从比部署低3.6pp）；而Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini、Llama-3.1-8B表现出相反的“部署谨慎”（评估vs部署的拒答效应为-9到-20pp）。OLMo-3基座模型也呈现部署谨慎模式，说明对齐阶段是行为反转的关键。Llama-3.1-70B保留了方向但幅度减弱，排除了“小模型效应”。但跨家族异质性依赖于评判器选择。

Conclusion: 模型的安全行为在评估与部署之间存在系统性分歧，且方向因模型家族而异，这严重削弱了当前安全基准对真实部署行为的预测有效性。研究者需要警惕这种上下文依赖性，并应考虑评判器本身的框架敏感性。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks are routinely treated as evidence about how a language model will behave once deployed, but this inference is fragile if behavior depends on whether a prompt looks like an evaluation. We define evaluation-context divergence as an observable within-item change in behavior induced by framing a fixed task as an evaluation, a live deployment interaction, or a neutral request, and present a paired-prompt protocol that measures it in open-weight LLMs while controlling for paraphrase variation, benchmark familiarity, and judge framing-sensitivity.
  Across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four open-weight families plus a matched OLMo-3 base/instruct ablation ($20$ paired items, $840$ generations per checkpoint), we find striking heterogeneity. OLMo-3-Instruct alone is eval-cautious -- evaluation framing raises refusal vs. neutral by $11.8$pp ($p=0.007$) and reduces harmful compliance vs. deployment by $3.6$pp ($p=0.024$, $0/20$ items inverted) -- while Mistral-Small-3.2, Phi-3.5-mini, and Llama-3.1-8B are deployment-cautious}, with marginal eval-vs-deployment refusal effects of $-9$ to $-20$pp. The matched OLMo-3 base also exhibits the deployment-cautious pattern, identifying alignment as the inversion stage; within Llama-3.1, the $70$B model preserves direction with attenuated magnitude, ruling out a simple ``small-model effect that reverses at scale.'' One caveat: the cross-family heterogeneity is judge-dependent. Re-judging with a different-family safety classifier (Llama-Guard-3-8B) preserves the within-OLMo eval-cautious direction but flattens the cross-family contrast, indicating that the two judges operationalize distinct constructs.

</details>


### [40] [Don't Lose Focus: Activation Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06342)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SKOP方法，通过保持关键令牌的注意力模式、约束不重要的注意力重路由，在保持95%以上引导效果的同时，将效用损失降低5-7倍，尤其在长上下文检索场景中表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导（Activation Steering）方法在控制LLM行为时，常因注意力重路由（attention rerouting）导致推理和检索性能下降。作者旨在解决这一引导-效用权衡问题。

Method: 提出SKOP（Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections），通过关键正交投影约束对焦点令牌（focus tokens）的注意力模式不被破坏，仅允许非重要尾部令牌间的注意力重分布，从而在保持引导效果的同时减少效用损失。

Result: 在多个引导基准上，SKOP实现了最优的引导-效用权衡：效用损失降低5-7倍，同时保留超过95%的原始引导效果。在长上下文检索场景中，SKOP通过避免注意力重路由保持了稳健性能。

Conclusion: 注意力重路由是导致激活引导方法性能下降的主要原因，SKOP通过保护焦点令牌的注意力模式有效缓解了这一问题，为LLM行为控制提供了更优的引导-效用平衡方案。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behaviour towards target behaviour by intervening in internal representations, yet it often degrades reasoning and retrieval performance. We argue that a primary cause of this trade-off is attention rerouting: steering vectors alter query-key matching, shifting attention away from contextually important tokens toward less informative ones. To address this, we propose Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections (SKOP), a steering method that constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy. SKOP achieves this by preserving attention patterns on a small set of focus tokens the model relies on for reasoning and retrieval, while allowing redistribution among less critical tail tokens. Across multiple steering benchmarks, we show that SKOP achieves the best joint steering-utility trade-off, reducing utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Our results further suggest that, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP can maintain robust performance by avoiding attention rerouting.

</details>


### [41] [SEQUOR: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Realistic Constraint Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06353)
*Beatriz Canaverde,Duarte M. Alves,José Pombal,Giuseppe Attanasio,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEQUOR是一个自动基准测试，用于评估长多轮对话中模型遵循用户指令约束的能力。实验发现，即使仅遵循单一约束，随着对话增长模型准确率下降超11%；多约束同时遵循时下降超40%；约束动态添加或替换时下降超9%，表明当前模型在多轮对话指令跟随方面仍存在显著困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令跟随基准大多聚焦于单轮或短多轮场景，缺乏对长时多轮对话中指令跟随能力的评估，无法衡量模型在用户不断细化、修改或矛盾指令下的表现。

Method: 提出SEQUOR基准，通过模拟基于persona驱动的交互，从真实对话中提取约束，构建长多轮对话场景，评估模型在不同约束条件（单约束、多约束、动态增/替换约束）下的指令跟随准确率。

Result: 单约束下准确率随对话增长下降超11%；多约束同时遵循时准确率下降超40%；约束动态变化时准确率下降超9%。

Conclusion: 当前模型在多轮对话中仍难以稳定遵循用户指令，SEQUOR为更好衡量助手指令跟随能力提供了有效评估手段。

Abstract: In a conversation, a helpful assistant must reliably follow user directives, even as they refine, modify, or contradict earlier requests. Yet most instruction-following benchmarks focus on single-turn or short multi-turn scenarios, leaving open how well models handle long-horizon instruction-following tasks. To bridge this gap, we present SEQUOR, an automatic benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in long multi-turn conversations. SEQUOR consists of simulated persona-driven interactions built with constraints extracted from real-world conversations. Our results show that even when following a single constraint, instruction-following accuracy consistently decreases as the conversation grows longer, with drops exceeding 11%. This decline becomes larger when models have to follow multiple constraints simultaneously, reducing their accuracy by over 40%. In scenarios where constraints are added or replaced at arbitrary points of the conversation, model accuracy decreases by more than 9%. Taken together, our results reveal that current models still struggle to follow user instructions in multi-turn conversations, and provide a way for better measuring instruction-following capabilities in assistants.

</details>


### [42] [GATHER: Convergence-Centric Hyper-Entity Retrieval for Zero-Shot Cell-Type Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06403)
*Zhonghui Zhang,Feng Jiang,Shaowei Qin,Jiahao Zhao,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GATHER，一种面向超实体查询的收敛中心检索器，通过全局多源图遍历识别拓扑收敛节点，在不依赖LLM的情况下高效实现零样本单细胞类型注释，在两个数据集上以单次LLM调用超越现有KG-RAG基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于知识图谱的RAG方法在零样本单细胞类型注释任务中采用局部实体遍历策略，但每个查询包含数十至数百个基因，单个基因不具决定性，只有基因共现模式才能确定细胞类型。这种"超实体查询"本质上挑战了基于单个基因的局部推理方法，导致扩展性差且LLM调用成本高昂。

Method: GATHER采用收敛中心检索范式，对输入基因集合执行全局多源图遍历，识别拓扑收敛节点（即可从多个输入基因共同到达的节点），并结合节点重要性和路径重要性评分进行证据筛选，整个检索过程无需LLM参与。同时构建了细胞中心生物知识图谱VCKG。

Result: 在Immune和Lung两个数据集上，GATHER分别取得27.45%和59.64%的精确匹配准确率，超越ToG、ToG-2、RoG、PoG等强基线方法，且每个样本仅需1次LLM调用，而基线方法需要2-61次。

Conclusion: 收敛节点能够将多实体信号压缩为紧凑的高信息量证据，每条证据的信息密度高于多跳路径，为局部实体推理提供了高效的全局替代方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot single-cell cell-type annotation aims to determine a cell's type from a given set of expressed genes without any training. Existing knowledge-graph-based RAG approaches retrieve evidence by expanding from source entities and relying on iterative LLM reasoning. However, in this setting each query contains tens to hundreds of genes, where no single gene is decisive and the label emerges only from their collective co-occurrence. Such hyper-entity queries fundamentally challenge local, entity-wise exploration strategies, which reason from individual genes, leading to poor scalability and substantial LLM cost. We propose GATHER (Graph-Aware Traversal with Hyper-Entity Retrieval), a convergence-centric retriever tailored to hyper-entity queries. It performs global multi-source graph traversal and identifies topological convergence points -- nodes jointly reachable from many input genes. These convergence nodes act as high-information hyper-entities that capture entity synergy. By incorporating node- and path-importance scoring, GATHER selects informative evidence entirely without LLM involvement during retrieval. Instantiated on a self-constructed cell-centric biological knowledge graph (VCKG), GATHER outperforms strong KG-RAG baselines (ToG, ToG-2, RoG, PoG) on two datasets (Immune and Lung), achieving the highest exact-match accuracy (27.45% and 59.64%) with only a single LLM call per sample, compared to 2--61 calls for KG-RAG baselines. Our results demonstrate that convergence nodes compress multi-entity signals into compact, high-information evidence that conveys more per item than multi-hop paths, providing an efficient global alternative to local entity-wise reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [MiA-Signature: Approximating Global Activation for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06416)
*Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Mo Yu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文借鉴认知科学中“全局点火”与意识通达的理论，提出Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)概念——通过子模函数选择高层概念对全局激活模式进行压缩表示，并可选地通过工作记忆轻量迭代优化。将该压缩信号作为条件注入RAG和智能体系统，在多项长上下文理解任务中取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学研究表明，可报告的意识通达与分布式记忆系统上的全局点火相关，但个体无法直接访问或枚举所有激活内容，说明认知可能依赖于一种紧凑表示来近似全局激活对下游处理的影响。受此启发，本文旨在设计一种压缩但信息丰富的激活签名，以在计算可行范围内近似完整激活状态的效果。

Method: 提出Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)：1）基于子模函数从高维激活空间中选择能覆盖上下文空间的高层概念；2）可选地利用工作记忆机制进行轻量迭代更新以精炼签名；3）将生成的MiA-Signature作为条件信号注入下游系统（如RAG和智能体系统）。

Result: 将MiA-Signature集成到RAG和智能体系统中，在多项长上下文理解任务上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MiA-Signature作为一种受认知科学启发的压缩全局激活表示，能够在保持计算可处理性的同时有效近似完整激活状态的效果，为提升长上下文理解任务提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: A growing body of work in cognitive science suggests that reportable conscious access is associated with \emph{global ignition} over distributed memory systems, while such activation is only partially accessible as individuals cannot directly access or enumerate all activated contents. This tension suggests a plausible mechanism that cognition may rely on a compact representation that approximates the global influence of activation on downstream processing. Inspired by this idea, we introduce the concept of \textbf{Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)}, a compressed representation of the global activation pattern induced by a query. In LLM systems, this is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, optionally refined through lightweight iterative updates using working memory. The resulting MiA-Signature serves as a conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks.

</details>


### [44] [From 124 Million Tokens to 1,021 Neologisms: A Large-Scale Pipeline for Automatic Neologism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06426)
*Diego Rossini,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展、模块化的自动新词检测流水线，结合规则过滤与LLM分类，在5.27亿条Reddit帖子中从1.246亿唯一词元中筛选出1,021个候选词，经人工验证599个（58.7%）为真正的新词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语料中自动检测新词面临挑战，现有方法难以平衡效率和准确性，且缺乏系统的分类框架和可复现的标准化流程。

Method: 结合基于规则（语法与非语法形态学）的过滤器与多个LLM分类器的投票机制，构建四分类方案（新词、实体、外来词、无关），先通过规则将1.246亿唯一词元缩减至1,021个候选，再由多个LLM独立分类并多数投票，最后人工验证。

Result: 从5.27亿条Reddit帖子（2005-2024）的1.246亿唯一词元中，经超过99.99%的缩减得到1,021个候选词。多个LLM分类存在显著分歧，人工验证确认599个（58.7%）为真正的新词创新。

Conclusion: 该流水线在大规模新词检测中具有可扩展性和模块化优势，但LLM间的分类不一致性表明该任务仍有挑战；公开的代码和标注数据为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: We present a scalable, modular pipeline for automatic neologism detection that combines rule-based filtering with LLM classification. The pipeline is grounded in two complementary word-formation frameworks, grammatical and extra-grammatical morphology, which jointly define the scope of what counts as a neologism and inform a four-class classification scheme (neologism, entity, foreign, none). While designed to be modular and transferable at the architectural level, the pipeline is instantiated on 527 million English-language Reddit posts spanning 2005-2024. From this corpus, we extract 124.6 million unique tokens and reduce them by over 99.99% to yield 1,021 neologism candidates, a set small enough for manual expert verification. Multiple LLMs independently classify each candidate via majority vote, with a final verification step, revealing substantial cross-model disagreement and highlighting the challenge of operationalizing neologism detection at scale. Manual annotation of all 1,021 candidates confirms that 599 (58.7%) are genuine lexical innovations. The pipeline code, vocabulary compilation scripts, and the annotated candidate list are available at https://github.com/DiegoRossini/neologism-pipeline.

</details>


### [45] [COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435)
*Vimala Balakrishnan,Lee Zing Hii,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了文本和语言特征（如词双元组、词性分布等）在假新闻检测中的应用。在COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集上，使用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林等传统机器学习方法进行实验。随机森林表现最佳，SVM紧随其后。文本和语言特征单独使用时均能提升检测效果，但组合使用未见显著提升。研究表明传统机器学习方法可有效利用这些特征进行假新闻检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管有实证证据表明文本和语言特征有助于区分真假新闻，但这类内容特征在假新闻检测中的研究仍然不足，因此本研究旨在系统性地探究这些特征的有效性。

Method: 使用COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集，选取词双元组和词性分布等文本与语言特征，采用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林五种传统机器学习模型进行实验。

Result: 随机森林在所有设置下表现最佳，支持向量机紧随其后。文本和语言特征单独使用时能提升检测效果，但组合使用未见显著提升。双元组与词性标签的使用效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 文本和语言特征可成功应用于传统机器学习方法的假新闻检测，无需依赖深度学习。研究同时揭示了不同特征组合及模型之间的性能差异。

Abstract: The use of content features, particularly textual and linguistic for fake news detection is under-researched, despite empirical evidence showing the features could contribute to differentiating real and fake news. To this end, this study investigates a selection of content features such as word bigrams, part of speech distribution etc. to improve fake news detection. We performed a series of experiments on a new dataset gathered during the COVID-19 pandemic and using Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbor, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. Random Forest yielded the best results, followed closely by Support Vector Machine, across all setups. In general, both the textual and linguistic features were found to improve fake news detection when used separately, however, combining them into a single model did not improve the detection significantly. Differences were also noted between the use of bigrams and part of speech tags. The study shows that textual and linguistic features can be used successfully in detecting fake news using the traditional machine learning approach as opposed to deep learning.

</details>


### [46] [Towards Emotion Consistency Analysis of Large Language Models in Emotional Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06476)
*Sneha Oram,Ojaswita Bhushan,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLMs在情感对话中对自身生成回复的一致性，发现模型在面对包含错误前提的查询时表现不佳，尤其对中等情感内容更为脆弱，且注意机制存在从评估到生成的优先级转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在情感驱动对话中对其自身生成回复的一致性，揭示模型在面对含有错误前提的情感性查询时的脆弱性。

Method: 将LLM生成的文本作为查询重新输入同一模型，评估其回复一致性；涉及三种包含错误预设的虚假声明查询（强度递增），覆盖极端和中等两个情感维度；分析了三个模型（Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT4o-mini、Mistral-7B）的表现，并进行了注意力分数分析。

Result: LLMs在情感对话一致性测试中表现低于平均水平，对查询中嵌入的错误信念敏感，中等情感内容下尤为明显；注意力分数分析显示模型优先级从评估性转向生成性。

Conclusion: 该发现对LLMs在高风险、情感敏感场景中的部署提出了重要考量。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct an analysis to examine the consistency of Large Language Models (LLMs) with respect to their own generated responses in an emotionally-driven conversational context. Specifically, the text generated by LLM is framed as a query to the same model, and its responses are subsequently assessed. This is performed with three queries across two dimensions of extreme and moderate emotions. The three queries are, in particular, false claim queries that contain inherently wrong assumptions (false presuppositions) in increasing order of intensity. Two commercial models, Claude-3.5-haiku, GPT4o-mini, and a medium-sized model, Mistral-7B, are considered in the study. Our findings indicate that LLMs exhibit below-average performance and remain vulnerable to false beliefs embedded within queries. This susceptibility is especially pronounced for moderate emotional content. Furthermore, an extended attention-score-based analysis highlights a shift in models' priority from evaluative to generative. The results raise important considerations for LLMs' deployment in high-stakes, emotionally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [47] [Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06546)
*Bowen Peng,Théo Gigant,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Token-Superposition Training (TST) 是一种无需修改模型架构、优化器或并行策略的预训练加速方法，通过将多个连续token合成一个"bag"并使用多热交叉熵损失进行高效训练，再恢复为标准训练，在10B参数规模下可实现高达2.5倍的预训练时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练在扩展时计算效率和数据吞吐量往往受限，现有加速方法通常需要对并行策略、优化器或模型架构进行复杂且侵入式的修改，因此需要一种简单、即插即用且无需修改系统组件的加速方案。

Method: TST包含两个阶段：(i) 叠加阶段（Superposition Phase），将多个连续token合并为一个"bag"，并使用多热交叉熵损失（Multi-hot Cross-Entropy, MCE）进行高效训练；(ii) 恢复阶段（Recovery Phase），恢复到标准训练方式。该方法不修改并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构，是一种即插即用的drop-in方法。

Result: 在270M、600M参数规模上广泛验证，并在3B和10B A1B混合专家模型上验证，TST在各种设置下均具有高度鲁棒性。在等损失条件下，TST在10B A1B规模上实现了高达2.5倍的预训练总时间缩减，且在损失和下游评估指标上持续优于基线。

Conclusion: TST是一种简单高效、即插即用的预训练加速方法，能够在保持甚至提升模型性能的同时，显著降低预训练时间，证明了token叠加训练策略在大规模语言模型预训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [48] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cola DLM 是一种层次化潜在扩散语言模型，通过信息分解先学习文本到潜在空间的映射，再建模全局语义先验，最后条件解码生成文本。该模型实现了非自回归的灵活生成，在~2B参数规模和约2000 EFLOPs计算量下验证了其扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归语言模型受限于固定的从左到右生成顺序，而现有替代方法在生成效率、可扩展表示学习和全局语义建模方面难以兼顾，因此需要一种更灵活的非自回归文本生成范式。

Method: Cola DLM 包含三个核心组件：1) Text VAE 学习稳定的文本到潜在空间的映射；2) 块因果 DiT (Block-causal Diffusion Transformer) 在连续潜在空间中建模全局语义先验；3) 条件解码器生成文本。扩散过程在潜在空间中进行先验传输而非 token 级别的观测恢复，实现全局语义组织与局部文本实现的分离。

Result: 在4个研究问题、8个基准、严格匹配~2B参数的自回归和LLaDA基线以及约2000 EFLOPs的缩放曲线实验中，验证了Cola DLM的有效配置和强扩展行为。

Conclusion: 层次化连续潜在先验建模可以成为严格的 token 级语言建模的一种原则性替代方案，生成质量和扩展行为比似然更能反映模型能力，并为统一建模离散文本和连续模态提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [49] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lighthouse Attention，一种基于对称分层选择的次二次注意力训练算法，通过可移除的压缩-解压缩机制和两阶段训练策略，在保持因果性的同时加速Transformer预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准缩放点积注意力（SDPA）在极端序列长度下存在二次时间和内存瓶颈，限制了因果Transformer的训练效率。

Method: (i) 次二次的分层预处理和后处理步骤，对序列进行自适应压缩和解压缩。(ii) 对称压缩策略，同时池化查询、键和值，保持从左到右的因果性，提升并行度。(iii) 两阶段训练：大部分时间用Lighthouse Attention预训练，最后短时间恢复为完整注意力模型。

Result: 在小型LLM预训练实验中，相比全注意力训练（其他设置相同），Lighthouse Attention实现了更快的总训练时间和更低的最终损失（恢复阶段后）。

Conclusion: Lighthouse Attention是一种有效的训练加速方法，可无缝包装在普通SDPA外部，训练结束时轻松移除，且梯度自由（无需处理复杂的反向传播内核）。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>


### [50] [Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06594)
*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无参考报告的远程认知康复场景中，自动化生成临床报告的方法。对比了基于规则模板的系统（编码言语治疗领域知识）和零样本GPT-4方法。由8位言语治疗师通过九维度问卷评估。结果表明模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上更优，GPT-4输出更简洁。基于评估反馈提出八条临床报告系统设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知矫正治疗需求增长，而言语治疗师资源有限，推动了远程康复工具的应用。这些系统产生大量交互数据，临床医生难以高效审查，亟需自动化临床报告生成方法。

Method: 对比两种方法：(1) 基于规则的模板系统，编码言语治疗领域知识为显式决策规则和验证模板；(2) 零样本LLM方法（GPT-4）。两者使用相同的预提取、专家验证的结构化变量。由8位言语治疗师和应届毕业生通过九维度问卷评估。

Result: 模板系统在流体性、连贯性和结果呈现上得分更高；GPT-4输出更简洁。各评估维度存在一致的方向性差异，但校正后均未达到统计显著性。基于反馈提炼出八条设计建议。

Conclusion: 临床可靠性与语言质量之间存在明确权衡。本研究提供了低资源临床自然语言生成中结合专家启发、分类驱动生成和多维人工评估的可复现方法论，展示了受控比较如何指导生成式AI在医疗中的负责任的采用。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [51] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSD统一框架，系统研究自蒸馏在自回归大语言模型中的应用。通过整合多教师一致、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪等互补机制，在六个基准和六个模型上揭示了自蒸馏何时有效、哪些组件驱动改进以及组件间如何交互。基于这些见解构建的UniSD-full集成管道比基础模型提升5.4分，比最强基线提升2.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏为大语言模型提供了一条无需依赖更强外部教师的适配路径，但自回归LLM中的自蒸馏存在挑战：自生成轨迹是自由形式的、正确性依赖任务、合理的理由仍可能提供不稳定或不可靠的监督。现有方法主要检查孤立的设计选择，其有效性、作用和交互尚不清楚。

Method: 提出UniSD统一框架，整合多教师一致（multi-teacher agreement）、EMA教师稳定化（EMA teacher stabilization）、token级对比学习（token-level contrastive learning）、特征匹配（feature matching）和散度裁剪（divergence clipping）五种互补机制，在六个基准和六个模型（来自三个模型家族）上系统研究。

Result: UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动性能提升、组件间如何跨任务交互。集成管道UniSD-full比基础模型提升5.4分，比最强基线提升2.8分。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏是一种实用且可操控的高效LLM适配方法，无需依赖更强外部教师即可实现有效性能提升。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [52] [Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06619)
*Jan Fillies,Ronald E. Robertson,Jeffrey Hancock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了“Algospeak”（算法语言）的博弈动态，引入“多数可理解调制”（MUM）概念，通过可复现框架生成不同调制级别的变体，以COVID-19虚假信息为例，用七种语言模型评估可理解性与检测率，发现二者之间的特征性权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型同时介入内容生成与审核，导致Algospeak（规避算法检测的语言策略）与检测器之间的共同进化加剧。现有研究缺乏对这种动态的形式化建模和对“可理解性-可检测性”权衡的系统实证。

Method: 基于联合行动模型形式化Algospeak动态；引入MUM概念定义权衡阈值；基于现有分类法构建可复现框架，生成保持语义、可调调制级别的Algospeak变体；以COVID-19虚假信息构建700条参考数据集（20条基础句×5个调制级别×7种策略）；用七种语言模型进行意义恢复（可理解性）和虚假信息检测（可检测性）评估；对调制级别进行曲线拟合以估计MUM阈值。

Result: 揭示了可理解性与调制级别之间的特征性关系；通过曲线拟合估算了多数可理解调制（MUM）阈值；在不同策略和模型间进行了敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Algospeak背后的动态奠定了基础，并提供了可复用的框架、数据集和实验设置，有助于未来研究语言规避策略与检测系统之间的协同演化。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [53] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个可复现的LLM生成报告引文归因评估框架，从链接可用性、内容相关性和事实准确性三维度评估14个模型，发现即使最强模型事实准确率也仅39-77%，且随工具调用增加准确率下降42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成报告中的引文无法可靠验证，自引用方法存在偏差风险，RAG方法不验证源可访问性、相关性和事实一致性，缺乏系统性的引文归因评估框架。

Method: 提出基于AST解析器的引文提取与评估框架，通过检索实际引用内容实现闭环验证；构建三维度评估体系（Link Works/Relevant Content/Fact Check）；使用基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-judge评估器，经人工校准；对14个闭源和开源模型进行基准测试。

Result: 最强前沿模型链接有效性>94%，相关性>80%，但事实准确率仅39-77%；不到一半开源模型能一次性生成带引文报告；随工具调用从2次增至150次，事实准确率平均下降42%。

Conclusion: 表面引文质量与事实可靠性之间存在严重脱节，更多检索并不会带来更准确的引文，该框架为评估这种脱节提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [54] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StraTA（Strategic Trajectory Abstraction）是一个分层强化学习框架，通过引入显式的轨迹级策略抽象，将策略生成与动作执行联合训练，在ALFWorld（93.1%）、WebShop（84.2%）和SciWorld（63.5%）上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法多为纯反应式（reactive），在长轨迹决策中探索效率低、信用分配困难，缺乏显式的轨迹级策略引导。

Method: StraTA从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，后续动作以该策略为条件；采用分层GRPO风格的回滚设计联合训练策略生成与动作执行，并引入多样化策略回滚和关键自我评判机制。

Result: ALFWorld上成功率93.1%，WebShop上84.2%，SciWorld上总体得分63.5%，超越闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 引入显式轨迹级策略抽象能有效提升LLM智能体在长程决策任务中的样本效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [55] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO是一种专为模块化设计的MoE架构，通过文档级专家共享约束（同一文档内token共享专家池）实现领域级专家自动分组，在保持全模型性能的同时，仅使用25%专家即可达到1%的精度损失，远优于标准MoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MoE模型无法支持独立使用专家子集，在内存受限场景下部署大模型时性能严重下降，限制了稀疏模型在内存受限环境中的实用性。

Method: 提出EMO架构，核心思想是鼓励同领域token依赖相似专家。具体做法是约束同一文档内的token从共享的专家池中选择专家，不同文档可使用不同专家池。这一简单约束利用文档边界即可在预训练过程中自动形成连贯的专家分组。

Result: 预训练1B活跃参数/14B总参数的EMO模型（1T token）。完整模型性能与标准MoE持平。选择性使用25%（12.5%）专家时，仅造成1%（3%）的绝对性能下降，而标准MoE在同样条件下完全失效。专家子集呈现出语义级别（如数学、代码）的专门化。

Conclusion: EMO证明了迈向模块化、内存高效部署大型稀疏模型的可行路径，为可组合架构打开了新的机会。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>

### [56] [Policies for the Operation of an Ambulance Fleet under Uncertainty based on a New Preparedness Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05421)
*Vincent Guigues,Anton Kleywegt,Victor Hugo Nascimento*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的应急准备度量指标，用于优化医疗急救服务中的救护车调度与重分配决策，并通过真实数据验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 救护车管理面临两大核心决策挑战：选择哪辆救护车响应紧急呼叫（调度决策）以及救护车完成任务后应派往何处（重分配决策）。由于当前决策会影响未来未知紧急事件的响应能力，做出最优决策十分困难。现有方法缺乏一个能够有效量化系统应对未来紧急事件能力的统一度量。

Method: 提出一个新的"应急准备度量"（preparedness metric），用于量化急救医疗服务应对未来紧急事件的能力。每次需要做出调度或重分配决策时，通过求解一个易于处理的优化问题，利用该度量来指导决策。

Result: 基于某大城市真实急救医疗服务数据的实验结果显示，所提方法在性能上优于文献中提出的9种对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提的应急准备度量及其对应的优化方法能有效解决救护车调度与重分配问题，在实际数据上表现优于现有方法，为急救医疗服务管理提供了更优的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Two important decisions in the management of an ambulance fleet are ambulance selection decisions and ambulance reassignment decisions. Ambulance selection decisions determine what to do when an emergency call arrives (such as choosing what ambulance to dispatch to the emergency or putting the emergency in a queue of emergencies waiting for an ambulance to be dispatched). Ambulance reassignment decisions determine where to send an ambulance next when it has finished service for an emergency. Making good ambulance selection decisions and ambulance reassignment decisions is challenging because a decision made at a point in time affects the ability of the emergency medical service to respond to future emergencies (that are typically not known when the decision is made). We propose a new preparedness metric that quantifies the ability of the emergency medical service to respond to future emergencies. The preparedness metric can be used to make ambulance selection decisions and ambulance reassignment decisions by solving a tractable optimization problem each time that a decision has to be made. We compare the performance of the resulting method with 9 methods that have been proposed in the literature, based on data from a real emergency medical service for a large city.

</details>


### [57] [A Computationally Tractable Path-Planning Method for Airborne Wind Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05502)
*Manuel C. R. M. Fernandes,Fernando A. C. C. Fontes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对地面生成式高空风能系统（Groundgen AWES）的放线阶段，提出了一种基于Lissajous曲线参数优化的计算高效路径规划框架，通过将路径规划问题转化为非线性规划问题并加入曲率约束，在避免最优控制计算复杂度的同时最大化平均功率输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空风能系统（AWES）作为一种有前景的可再生能源技术，其横风飞行控制中参考轨迹的选择对能量产出至关重要。现有最优控制方法计算负担重，需要一种计算上更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个计算可处理的框架，将路径规划问题表述为非线性规划（NLP），优化Lissajous曲线的参数以最大化放线阶段的平均功率，同时加入曲率约束。

Result: 该方法提供了一种比现有最优控制和基于学习方法更高效的替代方案，能够在合理计算成本下实现功率最大化。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Lissajous曲线参数优化的路径规划方法能够有效解决Groundgen系统放线阶段的参考轨迹选择问题，在保证计算效率的同时实现功率最大化。

Abstract: Airborne Wind Energy Systems (AWES) have emerged as a promising renewable energy technology that exploits stronger, more consistent high-altitude winds via tethered airborne devices. Among the various concepts, crosswind systems, where efficient flight control is essential to maximise energy output, offer significant potential. This paper addresses the problem of reference selection for crosswind flight control, focusing on the design of power-maximising geometric flight paths for the reel-out phase of Groundgen systems.
  To overcome the computational challenges associated with optimal control approaches, a computationally tractable framework is proposed in which a path-planning problem is formulated as a nonlinear program. The method optimises the parameters of a Lissajous curve to maximise the average power production over the reel-out phase, while incorporating curvature constraints. The proposed approach provides an efficient alternative to existing optimal control and learning-based methods.

</details>


### [58] [An Exact Algorithm for Load-Dependent Traveling Salesman Problem for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Package Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05507)
*Deepak Prakash Kumar,Saurabh Belgaonkar,Sivakumar Rathinam,Swaroop Darbha,David W. Casbeer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对负载依赖型旅行商问题（LD-TSP），提出了一个新的数学规划模型。该问题中，旅行成本（或能量消耗）取决于车辆的当前负载，适用于包裹配送和城市空中交通等场景。主要贡献包括：（i）建立能量耗散模型并证明能量消耗与车辆质量和行驶距离线性相关；（ii）建立混合整数非线性规划模型，并通过松弛方法转化为混合整数线性规划。大量数值实验表明，对于最多50个目标的大多数实例，最优解能在1分钟内获得；在10分钟限制内未解决的实例，最优性差距低于13%。与三个基线公式及另一个相关问题的算法相比，本文提出的方法表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包裹配送和城市空中交通等实际场景中，车辆的能耗或旅行成本会随载重变化而变化（如无人机载重越重能耗越高），但传统旅行商问题（TSP）模型假设成本固定或仅与距离相关，无法准确刻画这一动态成本特性。因此，需要一种新的建模方法来解决负载依赖型TSP问题。

Method: (i) 建立能量耗散模型，证明能量消耗与车辆质量、行驶距离呈线性关系；(ii) 提出混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）模型，并设计一种新颖的松弛方法将其转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP），以便高效求解。

Result: 对于最多50个目标点的大多数实例，最优解在1分钟内获得；在10分钟求解时限内，未解决实例的最优性差距低于13%。与三个基线公式及一个相关问题的算法相比，本文方法在所有指标上均表现更优。

Our key contributions are: (i) formulating an energy dissipation model and proving energy expenditure depends linearly on vehicle mass and distance; and (ii) formulating a mixed-integer nonlinear programming formulation and providing a novel relaxation to obtain a mixed-integer linear program. Abstract: We study the optimal design of stealthy attacks against partially observed linear control systems. Abstract: A Russell graph measure (RGM) is one of the standard DEA models, but its efficiency measure is not well-defined--or has unacceptable properties--at the boundary of the nonnegative orthant. This is known as a boundary problem. Existing studies have tackled this issue; however, their models may fail to identify an efficient target or fail to satisfy some desirable properties of efficiency measures. In this paper, we incorporate a closer target setting approach into the RGM model with production trade-offs to overcome such issues. We demonstrate that the efficiency measure of the proposed model overcomes the boundary problem and has stronger properties than existing models. We also demonstrate that the efficiency scores of the proposed model can be computed by solving a series of LPs. We conduct a numerical experiment with a real-world dataset to illustrate how targets provided by our model are realistic compared with the existing model, which also suggests the validity of our model in applications.

Abstract: We propose an adaptive proximal gradient method for minimizing the sum of two functions, where one is a simple convex function, and the other belongs to one of the three classes: nonconvex smooth, convex nonsmooth, or convex smooth. For the convex setting, we further propose an accelerated variant. It retains a similar near-optimal convergence rate for the nonsmooth case and achieves an improved rate of order $\widetilde{O}\big(1/t^2 + σ/\sqrt{t}\big)$ for the smooth case, which is optimal up to logarithmic factors. Notably, we develop new techniques for controlling the effect of stochastic noise, which are applicable across all three problem classes in the stochastic setting and enable simplified analysis.

Abstract: This paper presents a homogenized topology optimization (TO) method for spatially optimizing cell-size distribution of triply-periodic minimal surface (TPMS) structures, with high accuracy in the optimized structural response after de-homogenization. To achieve this, we introduce a novel de-homogenization technique that directly minimizes the difference between the wavenumbers obtained from the target and actual size distributions. This minimization problem is efficiently solved as a typical Poisson's equation utilizing the discrete cosine transform. Then, we apply the proposed method to a stiffness maximization problem, to demonstrate its effectiveness in improving the structural response compared to the PM method. The proposed method successfully reduced the distortion of the de-homogenized structures compared to the PM method, leading to 0.8% difference in the strain energy compared to the homogenized model, as opposed to 63.6% difference in the PM method. The optimized structure from the proposed method shows a significant improvement in the strain energy by 50.1% compared to the uniform case in the FE analysis on the de-homogenized models, while the PM method results in a significant decrease of 45.8%. The experimental validation shows that the effective stiffness of the optimized structure from the proposed method is 54.2% higher than that of the uniform case, while the PM method results in a significant decrease by 77.3%. These results exhibit the proposed method effectively increases the accuracy of the de-homogenization, thereby maximizing the potential of the homogenized TO for the spatial cell-size optimization of TPMS structures.

Abstract: This paper discusses the computational resolution and presents numerical results for solving affine combinations of Heaviside composite optimization problems (abbreviated as A-HSCOPs) by a progressive integer programming (abbreviated as PIP) method. The characteristics of these problems are that the Heaviside functions, which appear in the objective and define the constraints, are discontinuous, and their mixed-signed combinations result in the overall objective lacking the matching semicontinuity needed for the optimization and in the feasible set being not necessarily closed. Added to these challenging properties is the nondifferentiability of the inner functions in the composition. In this paper, we propose resolutions to all these challenges by first an approximation to remedy the lack of semicontinuity in the objective and closedness in the constraints, followed by a progressive integer programming approach with successive decomposition to handle the intrinsically discrete nature of the Heaviside function. Convergence to the local optimizers of the given Heaviside optimization problem is established. The effectiveness of the overall solution strategy is supported by extensive computational experiments on the score-based and tree-based multiclass classification problems with precision constraints.

Abstract: We prove a general quantitative theorem on the asymptotic behavior of stochastic quasi-Fejér monotone sequences in a broad metric context. Concretely, our result explicitly constructs a rate of convergence for such process, both in mean and almost surely, under an abstract stochastic regularity assumption, derived from previous work of Kohlenbach, López-Acedo and Nicolae [Isr. J. Math. 232(1), pp. 261-297, 2019] on such notions in a deterministic context. Our notion of regularity extends and unifies many common conditions from the literature, such as generalized contractivity for self maps, weak sharp minima and error bounds for real-valued functions, uniform monotonicity and global metric subregularity for set-valued operators, related Polyak-Łojasiewicz or Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz conditions, as well as expected sharp growth as e.g. studied by Asi and Duchi [SIAM J. Optim. 29(3), pp. 2257-2290, 2019]. The rate is moreover highly uniform, depending only on very few data of the surrounding objects. We also discuss special cases which allow for the construction of fast rates in the form of linear non-asymptotic guarantees. We conclude by presenting three concrete methods from stochastic approximation where our results yield new rates of convergence, including the classical example of the stochastic proximal point method, a randomized variant of the Krasnoselskii-Mann scheme for solving stochastic fixed-point equations, and a Busemann subgradient method recently introduced by Goodwin, Lewis, López-Acedo and Nicolae [Math. Program., to appear], all of which make use of our metric generality by being formulated over complete geodesic metric spaces of nonpositive curvature.

</details>


### [67] [Second-Order Bilevel Optimization with Accelerated Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06431)
*Sheng Yang,Chengchang Liu,Lesi Chen,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸-强凸双层优化问题，提出了完全二阶双层近似方法（FSBA）及其懒惰变体（LFSBA），分别达到了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$的迭代复杂度和更好的计算复杂度，并将类似思想扩展到极小极大优化中提出LMCN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二阶方法在非凸-强凸双层优化中的应用，探索其能否相比一阶方法实现加速收敛，并解决二阶方法计算开销大的问题。

Method: 提出完全二阶双层近似方法（FSBA），并设计懒惰变体LFSBA以复用二阶信息降低计算成本；将类似思想应用于非凸强凹极小极大优化，提出LMCN方法。

Result: FSBA达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$的迭代复杂度以找到$(ε, \mathcal{O}(\sqrtε))$二阶驻点；LFSBA的计算复杂度比FSBA降低$\sqrt{d}$倍；LMCN相比现有二阶方法具有更好的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 二阶方法在双层优化中能比一阶方法实现加速收敛，且通过懒惰策略可显著降低计算开销，该思路对极小极大优化同样有效。

Abstract: This paper studies second-order methods for nonconvex-strongly-convex bilevel optimization. We propose a novel fully second-order bilevel approximation method (FSBA) that achieves an iteration complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$ for finding the $(ε, \mathcal{O}(\sqrtε))$ second-order stationary point of the hyper-objective function. Our results demonstrate that second-order methods can achieve an accelerated convergence rate than first-order methods in bilevel optimization. To address the heavy computational cost associated with the second-order oracle, we introduce a lazy variant of FSBA, called LFSBA, which reuses second-order information across several iterations. We prove that LFSBA exhibits better computational complexity than FSBA by a factor of $\sqrt{d}$, where $d$ is the dimension of the problem. We also apply a similar idea to nonconvex strongly-concave minimax optimization and propose the lazy minimax cubic-regularized Newton (LMCN) method with better computational complexity compared to existing second-order methods.

</details>


### [68] [Confidentiality of Linear Control Systems with Quadratic Output Under Sensor Attacks [Extended Version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06493)
*Zeyad M. Manaa,Nathan van de Wouw,Michelle S. Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对平衡点处局部不可观测的二次输出线性控制系统，研究通过注入适当信号实现可观测性的状态估计问题，并构建了实现局部指数收敛的观测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统状态估计方法在系统于平衡点局部不可观测时失效，而实际系统中此类情况普遍存在，亟需开发能在不可观测条件下仍能有效估计状态的方法。

Method: 从攻击者视角，通过向测量信道注入适当信号来诱导可观测性；表征注入信号能否诱导可观测性的条件；在成功情形下构建观测器。

Result: 明确了注入信号诱导可观测性的条件，并构建了可实现状态估计局部指数收敛的观测器，仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 即使系统在平衡点局部不可观测，通过精心设计的信号注入策略仍可恢复可观测性，从而实现可靠的状态估计。

Abstract: We study the state estimation problem for linear control systems with quadratic outputs which are locally unobservable at the equilibrium. We show that, despite this inherent lack of observability, an adversary with sensor read and write capability can induce observability by injecting an appropriate signal into the measurement channel. Taking the role of an adversary, we characterize when an injected signal can or cannot induce observability and, in the successful case, construct an observer that achieves local exponential convergence of state estimates to the true states of the system. A simulation study demonstrates our results.

</details>


### [69] [Global self-optimizing control of batch processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06495)
*Chenchen Zhou,Hongxin Su,Xinhui Tang,Yi Cao,Shuang-hua Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对批过程的高度非线性和动态特性，扩展了全局自优化控制（gSOC）方法，通过向量化重构和快捷方法解决了因果约束和非凸优化难题，并在流加批反应器中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批过程因非稳态操作而表现出强非线性和动态特性，现有全局自优化控制（gSOC）方法因变量间的因果性无法直接应用于批过程，需要扩展gSOC以适应批过程的特殊需求。

Method: 提出向量化的gSOC问题表述，证明结构约束在向量化形式下是线性的；并设计了一种新颖的快捷方法，以高效寻找次优但更具透明性的解。

Result: 通过流加批反应器案例验证，使用具有重复结构的组合矩阵构造被控变量（CVs），得到了简单且易于实现的SOC方案，增强了实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了批过程中gSOC面临的结构约束和非凸优化挑战，所提出的快捷方法在保证性能的同时显著简化了控制方案，有利于实际工业应用。

Abstract: This work considers to achieve near-optimal operation for a class of batch processes by employing self-optimizing control (SOC). Comparing with a continuous one, a batch process exhibits stronger nonlinearity with dynamics because of the non-steady operation condition. This necessitates a global version of SOC to achieve satisfactory performance. Meanwhile, it also makes the existing global SOC (gSOC) not directly applicable to batch processes due to the causality amongst variables. Therefore, it is necessary to extend the original gSOC to batch processes. In addition to the nonconvexity challenge of the original gSOC problem, the new extension for batch processes has to face even more challenges. Particularly, the causality due to dynamics of batch processes brings in structural constraints on controlled variables (CVs), making a CV selection problem even more difficult. To address these challenges, the gSOC problem is recast in a vectorized formulation and it is proved that the structural constraints considered are linear in the vectorized formulation. Moreover, a novel shortcut method is proposed to efficiently find sub-optimal but more transparent solutions for this problem. The effectiveness of the new approach is validated through a case study of a fed-batch reactor, where CVs are constructed through a combination matrix with a repetitive structure, resulting in a simple SOC scheme. This simplicity facilitates the implementation of the SOC approach and enhances its practical applicability and robustness.

</details>


### [70] [Stochastic Non-Smooth Non-Convex Optimization with Decision-Dependent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06549)
*Chengchang Liu,Zongqi Wan,Haishan Ye,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了决策依赖分布下的随机零阶优化问题，在非光滑非凸、光滑和Hessian-Lipschitz三种设定下分别给出了SZO复杂度界，并在Hessian-Lipschitz情形下改进了已知最优的ε依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中（如在线学习、决策鲁棒优化等），采样分布往往依赖于当前决策变量，且只能获取带噪声的函数值，因此需要研究决策依赖分布下的随机零阶优化方法及其收敛性保证。

Method: 针对非光滑非凸设定，利用Goldstein平稳性条件建立收敛保证，SZO复杂度为O(d²δ⁻³ε⁻³)且每轮仅需单次SZO反馈；对光滑和Hessian-Lipschitz情形分别得到O(d²ε⁻⁶)和O(d²ε⁻⁹/²)的复杂度，后者将ε的依赖关系改进ε⁻¹/²。

Result: 非光滑非凸：O(d²δ⁻³ε⁻³) SZO复杂度且单次反馈；光滑：O(d²ε⁻⁶)；Hessian-Lipschitz：O(d²ε⁻⁹/²)，相比已有最优结果在ε依赖上改进ε⁻¹/²。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在决策依赖分布的非光滑非凸设定下给出显式收敛保证，并在Hessian-Lipschitz情形下达到了更优的复杂度上界。

Abstract: We study stochastic zeroth-order optimization with decision-dependent distributions, where the sampling law depends on the current decision and only noisy function values are available. For the non-smooth non-convex setting, we establish an explicit convergence guarantee for finding a $(δ,ε)$-Goldstein stationary point with stochastic zeroth-order oracle (SZO) complexity of $\mathcal{O}(d^2δ^{-3}ε^{-3})$. In addition, we show that the above complexity can be achieved with single SZO feedback per iteration. We further extend the analysis to smooth and Hessian-Lipschitz objectives, obtaining complexities $\mathcal{O}(d^2ε^{-6})$ and $\mathcal{O}(d^2ε^{-9/2})$, respectively. In the Hessian-Lipschitz case, this improves the best-known dependence on $ε$ for decision-dependent zeroth-order methods by a factor of $ε^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [71] [MTRBO: Multiple trust-region based Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06618)
*Sourav Das,Debjani Chakraborty,Pabitra Mitra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多信任区域的贝叶斯优化方法(MTRBO)，通过在一个信任区域内利用后验均值进行开发，在另一个信任区域内利用后验方差进行探索，解决了高维优化中搜索空间指数增长、目标异质性和采样预算低的问题，并在理论上证明了全局收敛性，在多种非凸高维测试函数和投资组合优化问题上表现优于当前最先进的信任区域贝叶斯优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化在高维问题中效率低下，主要原因包括搜索空间指数增长、目标函数的异质性以及有限的采样预算，亟需一种能够平衡局部开发与全局探索的高效高维BO方法。

Method: 提出MTRBO方法，以高斯过程作为先验，构建多个信任区域：一个信任区域基于后验均值函数在当前最优点附近进行局部开发（利用）；另一个信任区域基于后验方差函数在搜索空间中最不确定的区域进行探索。方法在这两个信任区域间自适应切换，并建立了理论的全局收敛性证明。

Result: 在多种非凸和高维测试函数上，MTRBO在采样预算（函数评估次数）内取得了优于其他先进信任区域贝叶斯优化算法的解质量。在投资组合优化这一实际应用场景中也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: MTRBO通过多信任区域机制有效平衡了探索与利用，在高维和非凸优化问题中具有显著的性能优势，并具备理论上的全局收敛保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is a popular framework for optimizing black-box functions. Despite its effectiveness, BO is often inefficient for high-dimensional problems due to the exponential growth of the search space, heterogeneity of the objective function, and low sampling budget. To overcome these issues, this work proposes a multiple trust region-based Bayesian optimization technique(MTRBO). A trust region is a localized region within which an optimization model is trusted to approximate the objective function accurately. Assuming a Gaussian process (GP) as a prior belief about the objective function and based on the posterior mean and variance functions, the method adaptively exploits near the promising current solution inside a trust region. Also explores the most uncertain region in the search space inside another trust region. The theoretical global convergence property of the proposed method is established. Then the work is benchmarked against other state-of-the-art trust-region-based Bayesian optimization algorithms, demonstrating superior performance on a variety of non-convex and high-dimensional test functions. The proposed method outperforms others in terms of solution quality within the sampling budget (the number of function evaluations). The proposed method is applied to the portfolio optimization problem to verify its applicability in real-world scenarios.

</details>

### [72] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释的注释者策略模型（APMs），通过标注行为学习注释者的内部安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可揭示分歧根源（操作失误、策略模糊或价值多元），并验证了其在建模准确性、反事实预测和已知策略恢复方面的有效性，展示了识别策略模糊和价值多元两大应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全策略依赖数据标注，但标注分歧普遍存在且来源多样（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元），区分这些根源对质量控制和策略设计至关重要，而直接询问注释者推理过程成本高且不可靠。

Method: 提出注释者策略模型（APMs），从标注行为中学习可解释的内部安全策略模型，无需额外标注负担即可使注释者的推理过程可视化和可比较。

Result: APMs能够准确建模注释者安全策略（>80%准确率），忠实预测反事实编辑下的反应，并在受控设置中恢复已知的策略差异。应用于LLM和人类标注，成功揭示了策略模糊和价值多元现象。

Conclusion: APMs能够支持更有针对性、更透明、更包容的AI安全策略设计，通过揭示分歧根源促进策略优化。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [73] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一個基於MoE++架構的推理聚焦混合專家模型，僅700M活躍參數/8B總參數，在數學和程式競賽基準上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528等更大模型。採用從預訓練開始的推理數據、四階段RL級聯訓練和創新的Markovian RSA測試時計算方法，在AIME'25達到91.9%準確率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持參數效率的同時，開發一個能與遠大規模開放權重推理模型競爭的推理專用模型，並證明全AMD計算平台能夠支援前沿AI模型的訓練。

Method: 採用MoE++架構（700M活躍/8B總參數）；預訓練至SFT全流程在AMD平台完成；使用答案保留修剪方案的推理數據；四階段RL級聯：推理熱身→RLVE-Gym課程（400任務）→數學/程式RL（測試時計算軌跡+合成程式環境）→行為RL；提出Markovian RSA，遞迴聚合平行推理軌跡，僅保留有限長度推理尾。

Result: 在AIME'25達91.9%，HMMT'25達89.6%；以<1B活躍參數匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528等多個數學和程式基準；與Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距顯著縮小。

Conclusion: ZAYA1-8B證明了小參數推理模型透過精心設計的架構、訓練策略和測試時計算方法可以達到與超大規模模型競爭的性能，同時全AMD平台的可行性也得到驗證。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [74] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Partial Evidence Bench基准测试，用于衡量企业Agent在受限检索环境中因访问控制导致答案看似完整但实际遗漏未授权证据的失败模式。包含72个任务、三个场景族，并评估四个维度的系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业Agent在受限检索、委托工作流和策略约束证据环境中运行时，即使访问控制正确执行，系统仍可能产出看似完整但实际遗漏未授权证据的答案，这种故障模式缺乏系统性的衡量方法。

Method: 构建Partial Evidence Bench确定性基准测试，包含尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应三个场景族共72个任务；采用ACL分区的语料库；提供oracle完整答案、oracle授权视图答案、oracle完整性判断和结构化差距报告oracle；从答案正确性、完整性感知、差距报告质量和非安全完整性行为四个维度评估系统。

Result: 基线测试表明静默过滤在所有场景族中均存在灾难性安全问题；显式失败并报告行为可在不将任务退化为简单弃权的情况下消除不安全完整性；初步大模型运行显示系统在过度声称完整性、保守地低估完整性或以企业可用形式报告不完整性方面存在模型依赖和场景敏感差异。

Conclusion: 该基准测试的广泛贡献在于使得治理关键的Agent失败模式可被衡量，无需人工评审或易受污染的静态语料库。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [75] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BALAR是一种无需微调的任务无关外循环算法，通过维护结构化信念、最大化期望互信息选择澄清问题、动态扩展状态表示，在三个基准测试（侦探案件、思维谜题、临床诊断）上分别显著提升准确率14.6%、38.5%和30.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在交互式场景中缺乏主动推理机制，无法有效判断缺失信息并决定下一步该问什么问题，导致对话系统反应式而非主动式。

Method: 提出BALAR算法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 维护潜在状态的结构化信念分布；(2) 通过最大化期望互信息选择最具信息量的澄清问题；(3) 当前状态表示不足时动态扩展状态空间。该算法无需微调，作为外循环与任意LLM配合使用。

Result: 在AR-Bench-DC（侦探案件）上准确率提升14.6%，AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）上提升38.5%，iCraft-MD（临床诊断）上提升30.5%，全面超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: BALAR作为任务无关的主动推理外循环算法，有效增强了LLM在多轮交互中的信息获取和推理能力，为构建主动式对话智能体提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [76] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM中的谄媚是社交对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失效，提出三条件框架（用户线索、对齐行为、认知损伤）和分类法，并倡导边界感知评估与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚简化为外部行为（如同意错误观点），忽略了认知完整性与社交对齐之间更微妙的边界失效，导致对现象的定义过于表面化。

Method: 提出三条件框架：用户表达线索（信念/偏好/自我概念）→ 模型向线索对齐 → 对齐行为损害认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。同时引入包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类法。

Result: 厘清了谄媚不应仅被视为"同意"，而是"取代独立认知判断的对齐行为"，提供了系统化的边界定义和分类框架。

Conclusion: 呼吁边界感知评估、结构化评估标准和缓解策略，并将这些建议置于不同谄媚观点的对比之中。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [77] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaTA是一个完全在本地硬件上运行的开源自动评分系统，专为采用LaTeX工作流的STEM课程设计。在OSU ME 373课程中部署后，每周为约200名学生评分，边际成本为零，评分错误率约0.02-0.04%，使用该系统的学生在期中考试中表现提升约11%，期末考试提升约8%，并在所有学习目标上报告了显著的自信心提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评分器虽有潜力减轻STEM高阶课程的评分负担，但现有部署多依赖第三方API，违反FERPA法规并带来数据风险，且需要大量作业修改。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线（摄取、分段、评分、报告），使用本地托管的开源链式思维LLM评分器（gpt-oss:120b），将学生作品与教师提供的参考答案进行比较，并应用带有二元逐项评分的YAML规则。

Result: 在OSU ME 373课程（约200名学生）上部署，使用一台Mac Studio，每份作业边际成本为零，每次提交耗时1-3分钟，评分错误率约0.02-0.04%。LaTA评分的班级期中考试成绩提升约11%，期末考试成绩提升约8%，所有学习目标的自信心提升显著（Δ≥+1.49 Likert点，p<10⁻²⁷）。

Conclusion: LaTA证明了完全本地化的开源LLM自动评分系统的可行性和有效性，能够在保护数据隐私的同时提升教学效果和学生信心。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [78] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"常数上下文技能学习"框架，将可重复的智能体工作流从提示词转移到模型权重中，通过轻量级任务族模块、确定性状态跟踪器和逐步SFT+在线RL训练，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上取得强性能，同时每轮提示词减少2-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人LLM智能体面临隐私-成本-能力的矛盾：云端模型能力强但暴露敏感中间上下文给外部API，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足；且两种场景都需要为长技能提示词和历史记录重复支付计算成本。

Method: 提出常数上下文技能学习框架：(1)将可重用工作流学习为轻量级任务族模块（上下文→权重转移）；(2)推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑状态块；(3)确定性跟踪器从任务进度渲染状态块并提供子目标奖励；(4)模块通过逐步SFT训练，再经在线RL精炼。

Result: Qwen3-8B上：ALFWorld未见任务成功率89.6%，WebShop成功率76.8%，SciWorld未见任务成功率66.4%。相比受控ReAct基线，每轮提示词减少2-7倍，性能匹配或超越已有强训练结果。

Conclusion: 将过程性上下文从提示词转移到权重中是可行的，在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低了提示词长度和隐私暴露风险，为隐私-成本-能力权衡提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [79] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出概率图模型框架，通过Pearl的do算子因果分析LLM安全机制中的文化人口统计偏见，发现标准公平性指标因未考虑上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏见，并揭示了东西方模型在因果拒绝率上的不同对齐趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM公平性评估主要基于观测性偏倚测量，但这种方法被测试数据集中与特定人口统计特征自然配对的固有毒性主题所混淆，无法区分真正的偏见与上下文毒性，因此需要因果审计框架来隔离文化人口统计注入的因果效应。

Method: 构建概率图模型(PGM)框架，应用Pearl的do-operator数学上隔离提示中注入文化人口统计的因果效应；在7个指令微调模型（美国Llama-3.1-8B/Gemma-2-9B、欧洲Mistral-7B-v0.3、阿联酋Falcon3-7B、中国Qwen2.5-7B/DeepSeek-7B、印度Airavata-7B）上，使用ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 观测偏倚与干预偏倚之间存在显著差异，标准公平性指标因未考虑上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏见；西方模型对特定人口群体表现出更高的因果拒绝率，而东方模型总体干预率较低但针对区域人口统计具有定向敏感性。

Conclusion: 人口统计敏感的过度触发会限制下游应用中的良性话语，需采用因果方法而非观测方法来准确评估和缓解LLM安全机制中的偏见。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B).
